ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u314	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, The National Tax Administration Act and the Introductory Act were adopted on 16 November 2016. We are now on the eve of the establishment of the National Tax Administration which consolidates separate branches: tax administration, tax control and the Customs Service. Government draft law amending the Law The provisions introducing the Act on National Tax Administration and the Act on National Tax Administration, print no.1297, aim to introduce the necessary adaptations to the Act on Tax on Goods and Services and the Law on Road Traffic. This is the main reason for this project. The laws concerning KAS were examined in Parliament at the same time as the law amending the VAT Act and the law amending the Road Traffic Law. The VAT novelization law has been adopted on 1 December 2016 and the Law amending the Law on Road Traffic was adopted on 30 November 2016. The laws on KAS were published on 2 December 2016. It was therefore not possible to harmonise the content of the laws adopted with the law not yet published. At the same time, the project proposes changes aimed at improving the establishment of the National Tax Administration. Prior appointment, even before 1 March 2017, of managers in newly created entities will improve the implementation of the National Tax Administration. On the other hand, it is necessary to retain the current appointments for management positions in tax offices until 31 May this year, since these offices continue their activities. The draft law adds amendments to remove doubts in the application of certain provisions. The proposed amendments to the Civil Service Act aim to achieve full compatibility between the KAS Act and the Civil Service Act. KAS employees are in the civil service corps, while higher management positions in the National Tax Information, tax administration, customs and treasury offices and tax offices are governed by the Civil Service Act. It is reasonable to abolish double checks at the confluence of retirement and clearance after the liquidation of the office. In such a case, the pension will be paid. It is not necessary that the accounts for the closure of the cancelled units are drawn up on 28 February this year. The proposed clarification of certain transitional provisions for the acquisition of tax control cases will eliminate the risk of legal disputes. The law on KAS is also amended to a small extent. There is no need for further maintenance of the institutions of value for officers in the KAS. Such a solution does not exist in other uniformed services. In the case of a transfer of an officer to a lower post, the previous allowance should be granted for 3 months instead of 6 months, which is equivalent to the civil service. It is proposed that the draft law should enter into force on the date of its publication, before 1 March 2017. It is extremely important that the legislative process is carried out efficiently. The Council of Ministers requests that this project be examined as soon as possible. Thank you. [[Applause]]

We proceed with the examination of item 12. of the agenda: First reading of the government draft law amending the Law on scientific degrees and title, and on grades and titles in the field of art and certain other laws (print No 1287). I ask the Undersecretary of State at the Ministry of Science and Higher Education of Mr Piotr Dardziński to present the justification for the bill.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u346	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The present bill, which is referred to as implementation PhDs, is a project that is part of the implementation of the Responsible Development Strategy and is designed to build staff that allows science to cooperate with business. As part of the preparation of innovative laws, the Ministry of Science and Higher Education conducted a very broad public consultation, which as one of the main problems that affects the low level of cooperation between science and business, indicated a lack of professional staff, which is able to understand mechanisms that operate both in the world of science and in the world of the economy. The proposal presented to you is intended to address this problem to a large extent. It enables the creation of staff, which will develop its career paths based on implementation work. This means that the doctoral studies carried out in this mode will be carried out simultaneously at the university or in the relevant scientific unit, which has the authority to doctorate, as well as at the employer, who will be obliged to hire a doctoral student. When these two conditions are met, the main promoter, who is a promoter at the university, and the auxiliary promoter, will be appointed to support the research work of the doctorate in the company. The research agenda shall be established by the entrepreneur, but in close cooperation with the scientific guardian who must ensure the appropriate scientific level of the doctorate being prepared. After establishing and fulfilling all these conditions, the PhD student will be able to apply for a scholarship, to take part in a scholarship program and to receive a minimum salary of PLN 2450. The scholarship program lasts 4 years, is evaluated every year. In the absence of milestones, the scholarship is withdrawn, and it is also possible to return it. The proposed law also provides for the possibility of obtaining equal rights for a doctor who is habilitated on the basis of implementation work. In this case, the proposed provisions refer to Article. 21a, which already makes it possible to obtain these rights equal to the degree of a doctor who is habilized to all people who come to Poland from abroad and have a degree of doctor. We would also like people who meet the right criteria to have experience in the five-year management of the research team and have significant scientific achievements to gain the power to strengthen Polish universities with scientific staff, which will appear at these universities with experience, which will be carried out in practice, with cooperation with entrepreneurs. It should be noted that these PhDs will be possible not only in enterprises but also in the institutions of the social environment, i.e. the implementation PhDs will also be possible in public institutions. He can be related not only to technical sciences, but also to social sciences, but also to humanities. In our proposed solutions, we have been inspired by the many years of experience of this type of PhDs, sometimes referred to as industrial PhDs, which operate especially in France and Denmark. They were introduced in the '70s and '80s. They are also very popular in some German lands. Experience shows that e.g. Denmark about 10% PhDs in implementation are in social sciences. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I ask you to accept a proposal which will significantly strengthen the potential for commercialization of Polish research, and thus contribute to the development of the Polish economy and Polish entrepreneurs, as well as strengthen the competences of scientific staff. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u348	Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! Minister! I have the honour to present the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice regarding the government draft law amending the Law on Science Degrees and Titles and on Art Degrees and Titles and some other laws, print No 1287. The project deals with the regulation of the academic career path based on conducting research aimed at applying their results in the economic or social sphere. The key solution contained in the project is the establishment of the implementation doctorate programme. This programme provides incentives to stimulate cooperation between the scientific community and the socio-economic environment. The current level of cooperation between the science and business sector in Poland is unsatisfactory. The problem of low levels of cooperation between these sectors is particularly clear and the share of R &amp; D expenditure in GDP is among the lowest in Europe. One of the factors limiting the scope of cooperation between the scientific community and the socio-economic environment is the lack of clearly defined opportunities for the development of academic careers on the basis of achievements in the implementation work. Successful people in the use of research or development results in the socio-economic environment do not have clearly defined principles for the development of their careers. This reduces the interest of the scientific community in implementation work. The new career path designed in the Act will enable you to obtain a doctoral degree or qualifications equivalent to those of a doctor who is habilised on the basis of the achievements obtained in the implementation work. Those who have been successful in applying research or development results could be, among others, included in the so-called. minimum staff, act as a promoter in doctoral courses, be employed as an extraordinary professor or obtain a professor's title. Such career opportunities are expected to provide incentives for those planning an academic career to target the implementation of research results. The establishment of the implementation doctorate is intended to provide additional incentives for young researchers to develop their careers on the basis of implementation activities. Increased interest in implementation work should translate into increased cooperation between scientific units and entities operating in their socio-economic environment, which in turn will contribute to the innovation of the Polish economy and to the improvement of the quality of life of society. The programme provides for the funding of costs related to doctoral studies in which training takes place in cooperation with the socio-economic environment, including the entrepreneur or other entity concerned by the specific area of research. And it should be noted that implementation PhDs have been successfully implemented in other Member States of the European Union for many years. They are implemented in Denmark, France, Germany and recently also at European Union level. Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! Minister! Secondly, it cannot be accepted that the granting of rights equivalent to the rights of a habilitated doctor should be done by the rector, the director of the scientific institute of the Polish Academy of Sciences or the director of the research institute. The role of collegial bodies in this area must not be undermined. ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u377	Thank you very much. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! The draft law amending the excise duty law aims primarily at eliminating irregularities related to the marketing of completely contaminated ethyl alcohol and at eliminating interpretation doubts concerning compulsory excise duty exemption for ethyl alcohol completely contaminated in Poland. The irregularities in the marketing of completely contaminated alcohol relate in particular to ethyl alcohol contaminated by the Hungarian method, since the Hungarian method is relatively easy to neutralise - the result is the disinfection of that ethyl alcohol. Decontaminated ethyl alcohol shall be used improperly for the purpose of applying excise duty exemption, e.g. such alcohol is consumed. The project aims to increase control over the marketing of ethyl alcohol completely contaminated by the ongoing collection of information on the quantity of alcohol purchased from other European Union countries. Under the amended arrangements, a person purchasing alcohol wholly contaminated from other countries of the European Union will be required, first, to register as a taxable person excise duty, second, to notify the intended acquisition of alcohol to the tax authority. In contrast, the elimination of interpretation doubts will be achieved by stating that in the territory of the country ethyl alcohol can only be completely contaminated with excise duty if it has been contaminated with a contaminating agent used in all Member States, so-called. Europolluter, or contaminating agents reported by Poland. At the same time, it will be clarified that in the acquisition of an intra-Community excise duty exemption, ethyl alcohol which is wholly contaminated with a europolluter or additional contaminating measures authorised by the Member State of the European Union in which the contamination occurs will be subject. In the case of imports of excise duty exemption, ethyl alcohol which is wholly contaminated with a europolluter or additional contaminating measures authorised by the Republic of Poland will be subject. The Customs Service estimates that misuse could have been used in 2014 ca. 2.7-3.3 million l of 100-percent alcohol. In 2015, it was about 5.5 million l of alcohol originating in Hungary. However, it should be borne in mind that these estimates have been made on the basis of the conclusions of the checks carried out and of the analyses carried out and can therefore be overestimated and therefore the impact of the changes will be assessed after 2 years of validity. In view of the above, it is justified to introduce the proposed amendments to the excise duty Act. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u378	Thank you very much, Minister. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. Mr Marek Polak, Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, will be the first to speak.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u379	Thank you very much. Dear Marshal! Ladies and gentlemen, ladies and gentlemen, It is my honor and great pleasure to present to the High House the position of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice as regards the government draft law amending the excise duty Act in print No 1288. The draft law submitted, as the Minister was polite to say, aims to eliminate interpretation doubts as regards the use of excise duty exemptions referred to in Council Directive 92/83/EEC and the European Commission Regulation of 21 February 2013 on mutual recognition of procedures for the total contamination of ethyl alcohol for the purposes of exemption from excise duty. The solutions proposed in the draft also allow for the continuous collection of information on the quantity of completely contaminated ethyl alcohol which is the subject of intra-Community acquisitions. The provision of continuous monitoring on this issue will be carried out by imposing mandatory excise duties on the acquiring entity as a taxable person and reporting the proposed acquisition to the competent tax authority. The registration will also require the identification of the addresses of the places of business and the estimated monthly quantity of alcohol consumed. It should be stressed here that completely contaminated ethyl alcohol in any of the Member States of the Union is compulsoryly exempt from excise duty and the changes made will not result in a tax obligation. One of the reasons for this regulation is that irregularities related to the disinfection of completely contaminated alcohol with products authorised by one of the Member States of the Union which are extremely easy to remove and do not ensure permanent contamination of ethyl alcohol or products produced on its basis not intended for human consumption. As a consequence, the clarification of the provisions of this Act will result in exemption from excise duty only from production, intra-Community acquisitions or imports of ethyl alcohol totally contaminated with only measures authorised in all Member States of the European Union, as well as additional contaminating measures authorised by the Member State in which the contamination takes place and additional measures authorised by our country. It is worth noting that the proposed changes will have no impact on the increase in expenditure in the public finance sector, nor on the reduction of revenue, since the changes concern the current excise exemptions and, as I said earlier, are more specific. To conclude, I would like to say that our club, after careful analysis of the proposed bill, is in favour of implementing the proposed amendments. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u380	Thank you very much, sir. I invite Mr Włodzimierz Nykiel, the Civic Platform Club.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u381	Dear Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour of presenting my position on behalf of the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club on the government draft law amending the excise duty law, print no. 1288. First of all, it must be stressed that European Union law provides for exemption from excise duty of completely contaminated ethyl alcohol, which is usually used in the cosmetic, pharmaceutical and chemical industries. The exemption from excise duty is governed by the Council Directive of 19 October 1992 on the harmonisation of the structure of excise duties on alcohol and alcoholic beverages. This directive is called the Alcohol Directive. The issue of the method of contamination is important in relation to these regulations. According to the rules, contamination can be carried out through the application of a uniform contaminating agent in all Member States - it is called: a Europolluter - or additional contaminating measures in the Member States. Some national contaminating measures, including in particular the Hungarian contaminater, are characterised by impermanence or are easy to remove, which facilitates tax fraud in the manner mentioned by the Minister. In Poland there are many such frauds. At the same time, in Poland courts allowed such an interpretation that ethyl alcohol could be contaminated in any way permitted in any Member State, including the Hungarian method. In this state of affairs, it should be pointed out that the regulations contained in this project are necessary and merit a positive assessment. They remove interpretational doubts that have grown against the existing wording. According to the regulations contained in the draft exemption, alcohol will be subject to a completely contaminated Europollutant, i.e. one that can be used in all European Union countries. In the event of intra-Community acquisitions, alcohol shall be released completely contaminated with additional measures authorised by the EU country in which the contamination occurs. Finally, in the case of production in Poland the exemption will be subject to ethyl alcohol contaminated with additional measures authorised by Poland, authorised by the Republic. What is most important here and what is the result of these regulations, is that as a consequence of these solutions in Poland cannot be released alcohol contaminated completely according to the Hungarian method, the one that is produced in our country. I don't have much time left to do the registration or the call. Anyway, as I said, this regulation deserves a positive assessment. Thank you. [[Applause]] [[Voice from the room: Bravo!]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u393	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Minister! I present a report by the Committee on Justice and Human Rights on the Government bill amending the Penal Code Act and some other laws, print No 1186. The Sejm at its 34th meeting on 26 January 2017 pursuant to Article. 39 (1) (a) 2 of the Rules of Procedure of the Sejm referred the above draft law to the Committee on Justice and Human Rights for consideration. The Commission set up a subcommittee and then the draft was discussed at the meetings of the Committee on Justice and Human Rights on 8 and 21 February 2017. The Commission proposes that the High Sejm adopt the attached draft law. High Izbo! I would like to remind you that this proposal is a government submission which implements two Directives of the European Parliament and of the Council: the Directive of 3 April 2014 on the freezing and confiscation of instrumentalities and benefits of crime in the European Union and partly the Directive of the European Parliament and of the Council of 12 August 2013 on attacks against information systems. The entry into force and implementation of the provisions of this essential Directive of 3 April 2014 should take place on 4 October 2016. This government submission is catching up on the delay that has taken place in implementing this very important directive, and criminal law has already been adapted to this directive in many countries of the European Union and not just the European Union. First of all, it must be emphasized that the forfeiture of property is defined in our constitution and can only happen if it results from the law and judicial decisions - in addition to this constitutional record about the forfeiture of things. Since the criminal codes have been in force, there have been rules on the loss of property, confiscation of property, including criminal codes and the current 1997 Criminal Code in Article. 45 and in the Tax Code in Article. 33. This solution leads to a new situation, new norms. The amendment adds the provision of Article. 44a, who says that in the event of a conviction for a crime from which the perpetrator achieved, at least indirectly, a significant asset - a significant value is more than 200,000. zł - the court may rule on the loss of an undertaking owned by the perpetrator or its equivalent if the undertaking was used to commit the offence or to conceal the advantage it has obtained. On the other hand, § 2 provides that, in the event of a conviction for an offence from which the perpetrator has obtained, inter alia, an advantage of considerable value, the court may decide that the non-owner of an undertaking of a natural person or his equivalent, if the undertaking has served to commit the offence or to conceal the advantage it has obtained, and that the owner of the undertaking would have the undertaking serve to commit or hide the offence or the benefit it has obtained, or provided for that possibility, he has agreed to do so. These rules are very important because they are new to the issue of forfeiture. That's what I call it. extended version. The point is that people who actually benefit from committing crimes and then place this property properly... We know of cases where they're often set up by other people, or they're from the nearest family. In such a case, in the event of a conviction for an offence from which the offence has been committed, even indirectly, an advantage of considerable value, or an offence from which it has been or could have been obtained, for example indirectly, a property benefit, punishable by a sentence of imprisonment, of which the upper limit is not less than 5 years, or committed in an organised group or in a relationship with a view to committing a crime, the property which the perpetrator has taken over or has obtained any title within a period of 5 years before the offence has been committed shall be regarded as an advantage. ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u395	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Criminals and organised criminal groups operate independently of existing borders. They increasingly obtain property in countries other than those mostly active in. It is well known that the so-called. Extending confiscation may be much more painful for the perpetrators of crime than imprisonment. Often criminals, especially economic criminals or treasurers, include imprisonment in the cost of their activities. They know that they will be able to continue to enjoy the proceeds of the estate after it has been completed. The need for effective tools to combat crime is becoming increasingly urgent. Inevitably depriving offenders of the benefits of crime is essential for the effective operation of criminal law. Of course, it is not enough just to deprive criminals of the benefits of preventive action, as is the punishment itself. Currently, criminal law does not have mechanisms to effectively and fully deprive criminals of illegally acquired assets and other benefits, which means that it is unable to effectively combat crime, especially the most dangerous organised crime. The forfeiture of the property of the perpetrators of crime shall apply. not only for repression by an additional ailment, but also for prevention, perhaps even more important. It is about depriving the perpetrators of crime of material benefits that enable them to continue their criminal activities. From a social point of view, it is also an act of justice against victims and against society to deprive the perpetrators of material benefits. Taking away the benefits of crime is not just a punishment, but it complements other penalties, including imprisonment. A single penalty may only be imposed if, for formal reasons, the application of other penalties, despite proving the offence, becomes impossible. The draft law amending the Penal Code Act and some other laws aims to introduce changes to Polish criminal law which significantly improve the functioning of mechanisms to deprive offenders of the benefits of criminal acts. This problem has also been addressed several times in the European Union. The Stockholm Programme adopted by the European Council for the period 2010 and 2014 to combat economic crime and corruption indicated that the effectiveness of confiscation of criminal assets should be improved and cooperation between asset recovery offices should be strengthened. This document also talks about increasing efficiency in identifying the assets of the perpetrators of crime. The proposals presented in this project include an increase in the efficiency of property losses created by crime. In the case of particularly dangerous and serious economic crimes committed by the company as a tool for criminal acts, the bill provides for the collapse of such an undertaking, whether or not it is the sole property of the perpetrator. In some situations, the proposal gives the possibility to rule on the loss of the proceeds of the crime despite the existence of obstacles to the conviction. In addition, the proposal envisages amending the implementing criminal law to facilitate the execution of the ordered forfeiture of the property benefit achieved by the criminal offence. ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u419	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen! Last Friday, February 17th, the 94th anniversary of the first Polish Quarter in Germany was solemnly celebrated. Former flagship mails, flowers, a call of memory, commemorative speeches - these were the celebrations connected with the 94th anniversary of the First District of the Association of Poles in Germany, which was organized on pl. Freedom in Opole. It is worth recalling the work of our compatriots, who more than 94 years ago joined the Polish Union in Germany. It's worth it, among other things. because the fight between the Polish Union in Germany for the rights of Poles living within the borders of the German state is still a wonderful lesson of identity care. That is why I would like to draw attention to some facts from the history of this great movement. Poles living within the borders of the German state wanted to preserve the tradition connected with the fatherhood, i.e. the land that they lived for dozens of years, and sometimes for hundreds of years, their tradition was Polishness. In order to function smoothly, the union created the so-called "union." The districts, and the headquarters of the first one were Opole. Shortly after the creation of the Polish Union in Germany, its activists began a conscious action aimed at permanent introduction of the names of Silesia Opolski and Opolszczyzna into the social circulation. It was an opposition to the names used in the European press: Polish Upper Silesia and German Upper Silesia. It was then that Edmund Osman wrote a poem in Opole, in which we read, among others.: You're mine, Opole. In you, my word! The word is invincible, like everything Polish. Behold, I stand in the land of the Fathers with a living word. My city - Opole - how opole! The fight for Polish names shows the extraordinary wisdom of the activists of the Polish Union in Germany, who knew that the country is a language. An important element of this care of Polishness was the activity of Polish priests. One of the most famous was Fr. Bolesław Domanski, who, in the first years of the Polish Union in Germany, visited the Opolskie villages with stubbornness at meetings and sermons, repeated: We are Poles. We must not forget that the activity of the Polish Union in Germany was interrupted during World War II. With the outbreak of World War II, the arrests of members began. On the basis of the Decree of the Council of Ministers for the Defence of the Third Reich of 27 February 1940, the union was delegated and its assets confiscated. Some of the activists were shot and some 1,200 members were imprisoned in concentration camps. The total number of persecuted is estimated to be at least 2 000. persons. The material losses incurred by the Polish national minority in Germany, caused by the confiscation of assets, are valued at at least 8.5 million Reichsmarek. Until now, Poles have not regained the official status of a national minority in Germany, which was taken from them by the Nazi authorities of the Third Reich. I believe that the long-lasting, long-term dialogue will eventually lead to the problem of the status of Poles in Germany as expected by our countrymen. Taking advantage of this opportunity, I would like to thank the members of the House for their heroic work for Polish citizens living in Germany. Ladies and gentlemen! I want to end my speech with words, with the wisdom of old Poles: Poland must be served always and everywhere, because it is up to this persistent, daily service which Poland will be in the future. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u437	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! This year for the 5th time we celebrate the day of memory of the cursed soldiers. It must have been decades before the historical truth about our heroes came out. For several decades, the subject of solid soldiers was a taboo topic that was not allowed to be spoken about. There would be no national drama if the Allies had not betrayed Poland in Yalta and Tehran. It is there that the great three have determined the post-war division of Europe into zones of influence. Truman, Churchill and Stalin have agreed on matters of subjugation to countries that will be occupied by them. Postwar Poland was in the Soviet sphere of influence. It was the others who decided for us and for us, but without us. Both the Polish government in London and the authorities of the Polish underground state were aware of the seriousness of the situation and the real threat to Poland's independence as a sovereign state. In this situation, the last commander of the Home Army General Leopold Okulicki issued an order to dissolve the Home Army and release soldiers from military oath so as not to expose them to repression by the new invaders. They were to become involved in everyday life in a new political reality. Only personnel were saved for possible armed conflict between the Allies and the Soviet Union. Repressions, prisons, exports to Siberia caused tens of thousands of former AK soldiers and the underworld of independence to return to the forest. They fought for the Polish national identity, free of foreign dictatorship and alien to us, Poles, style of government based on lawlessness and violence. High Izbo! The legendary commander has shown great foresight. The Vilnius Brigade of the AK Major Zygmunt Szendzielarz.Łupaszka. In September 1945, Mr Łupaszka arrived in Gdansk Pomerania. He organized a wide-ranging propaganda activity aimed at the communist apparatus of repression. The area of his activity was also Kociewie. Collaborators and traitors of the country were eliminated. The surrounding population felt more confident when she was aware of the presence of the 5th brigade's soldiers, who protected her from UB's self-will, arrests, props, or common robbers. Łukasz Cieplinski. Only on September 17, 1992, the Court of the Warsaw Military District annulled the criminal judgment of the district court in Warsaw. ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u445	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! February 28th this year We will celebrate the 205th anniversary of the death of a clergyman, a prominent politician and enlightened publicist Hugon Kołłątaj. Hugo Kołłątaj was born on 1 April 1750 in Volyn in a wealthy noble family. Initially, he received education in schools in Pińczów, then studied at the Krakow Academy, as well as in Vienna, Bologna and Rome. He got a PhD in philosophy, law and theology. In 1775, he was ordained priestly. Hugo Kołłłętaj was a co-worker of the National Education Commission, as well as a creator of a plan to expand schools across the country. In the framework of the reform at the Krakow Academy he introduced among others. lectures on natural sciences and Polish literature, gave access to science to students from the city, and also improved the academic level of the university. In 1779, he moved to Warsaw. There he gathered a group of publicists called Kuznica Kołłętajska. In 1786 Hugo Kołłętaj was awarded the Order of St. Stanisława, and a few years later he became a bachelor of the Order of the White Eagle. He was involved in the creation of the Constitution on 3 May. During the Four Year Sejm he was one of the most active activists of the Patriotic Party. In the hearing entitled: Stanisław Małachowski, crown referee. About the future Seym Anonymous of Letters, several of them prepared a programme of reforms just culminated in the Constitution on 3 May. After its adoption, he was appointed a sub-chancellor of the Crown. During the uprising of Kościuszkowski, Kołłątaj belonged to the National Supreme Council in which he took over the Treasury Department. After the loss of independence by Poland, he was arrested by Austrians. He was punished in prison in Olomouc and Josephstadt fortress. Released after many family procedures he went to Volyn, where he was the creator of Krzemieniecki High School. Suspected of contact with Napoleon was deported and held in Moscow by Russians. Between 1809 and 1812 he was a member of the actual Society of Warsaw's Friends of Sciences. He spent the last years of his life in the Duchy of Warsaw, where he died after a long illness. He was buried in Warsaw's Powązki, but the urn with his heart was deposited in the church in Wiśniowa, where his brother Rafał Kołłłątaj lived. Kołętaj in his life was guided by the slogan: "Polish people! Dare, for once, be a nation, and a nation truly free, and his works focused mainly on scientific subjects, especially history, philosophy and education. He criticized the outdated political and social structure of the Republic of Poland. Among the most important political works we can include above all the above-mentioned trial of Stanisław Małachowski... and the political law of the Polish people. Another great work of Kołłętaj was of a geographical nature. Written in the years 1802-1808, the critical collection of the principles of history about the origins of the human race, released only in 1842, was an excellent system of general geography, full of creative thoughts at that time. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u446	Mr. Marshal! High Seym! Yesterday we celebrated the 70th anniversary of the last guerrilla fight by the Major. Józef Kurasia Fire. It was surrounded by the KBW operating group in Ostrowsk k. New Market and there he fought the last fight. During the September campaign he fought in ranks 8 of Company 1. The Podhale Rifle Regiment. After his defeat, he managed to avoid slavery and returned to his hometown after a failed attempt to enter Hungary. After the formation of the Tatra Confederation in 1941, he became the head of the Waksmund post, choosing the pseudonym Eagle. The arrests of the most important activists of the Tatra Confederation at the beginning of 1942 led to its complete breakdown. In the spring of 1943, only the Kurasia group, which focused people working on the farm in Długa Hall near Turbacz, remained active from the whole organization. German confidants sent to the mountains to search for it in the area of Polana Gabrowska fell into an ambush and were killed. In retaliation, the Germans murdered his father, his wife, and his two-and-a-half-year-old son Kuras, and burned down the family house. After this tragedy, Kuras changed his nickname to Fire. In 1945, prior to the arrest, Fire returned to the forest, starting a fight with the authorities. He soon became the commander of the largest group of anti-communist guerrillas in the area. There were many former AK soldiers in her ranks. The chicken considered all those who with guns in their hands to be enemies of the unwanted system. Among them, NKVD soldiers and UB officers were among the most dangerous. His actions were also directed against state institutions and economic facilities and served the so-called. Discipline of society, e.g. by eliminating collaborators or common criminals. There were also various kinds of props and contributions. Soon for the PUBP at the New Market, the most important issue was the breaking up of the fire group. But it wasn't that easy. Kuras, who had great guerrilla experience, based on a well-developed network of informants and frequently changing the place of residence, effectively slipped away from militia manhunts. At the same time, he had great support in the rural environment. Finally, WOP troops were assigned to the fight to replace NKVD troops in the border service. An increasingly skilled propaganda campaign has also been launched to defy fire support in society. A person was even used as a captain. Juliana Zapala - the legendary Lamparta, who was persuaded to sign the appeal to his fellow Firemen, in which he encouraged them to reveal themselves. All of this has finally worked out. On February 10, 1947, the head of PUBP at Nowy Targ managed to recruit a new agent named Orientation. This soon filed a report on the location of the Fire Staff camp. This camp was located on the Common Millage located in the Kudłonia massif. At dawn on February 18th, Captain. Władysław Dworakowski, along with the KBW soldiers and UB officers, arrived and hit the camp. After a short fight, the firefighters withdrew. The branch was divided into small groups that found refuge in the surrounding villages and settlements. Hen in the company of his closest associates decided to wait first at Waksmund and then at the house of Józef Zagata in Ostrowsk. Another report of the Oriental House made the Zagata house on 21 February and then the neighbouring house was surrounded. During the fight, the cornered fire did not want to get into captivity, he shot himself in the temple. Wounded, brought to the hospital in Nowy Targ, he died in it on February 22, 1947. Before the National Day of Remembrance let us remember the heroic activity of Fire. Thank you.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-02-22-sejm-36-1.u447	The Metropolitan Act. The debate on the introduction of solutions for metropolitan areas in Poland has been taking place for two decades - in areas to become metropolitans, demographic, social and economic processes have gone so far that they require other institutional and legal solutions. The draft law on the metropolitan union in the Śląskie voivodship responds to the need to establish a metropolitan union in the Upper Silesia area, which will be guaranteed by the law of competence appropriate to the area of such character, and resolves doubts about public collective transport tasks. In view of the need to ensure the sustainable development of the remaining smaller functional areas, it is desirable to develop complementary tools in this area to the metropolitan law. The Council of Ministers will form a union and establish its boundaries in connection with the resignation from the delimitation. The new definition proposed is: the metropolitan union is an association of gminas of the Śląskie voivodship, characterized by strong functional links and advanced urbanization processes, located in a spatially coherent area, which lives at least 2 million inhabitants. The regulation will determine the name and seat of the union authorities - they will be able to join it with other municipalities from the Silesian Voivodeship, but without the possibility to perform. A regulation establishing a metropolitan union and establishing its borders will be issued at the request of the Minister responsible for public administration. The Minister's proposal will be drawn up on the basis of a proposal from the City Council of Katowice - the city of Katowice is the only municipality listed directly by the law which will be part of the metropolitan union. Proposal: the law, not the regulation, will regulate the application procedure and the required documents. The City Council of Katowice will apply to the Minister responsible for public administration through the Silesian Voivodeship. The governor shall, no later than 30 days from the date of receipt of the request, forward it together with his opinion to the minister responsible for public administration. The project also specifically regulates the mode of changing the boundaries of the metropolitan union. The Council of Ministers will take into account existing forms of cooperation between local government units. The draft law requires consultation of residents when the municipalities adopt resolutions on the establishment of a metropolitan union and the establishment of its borders. President Katowice will be the proxy for the creation of the union. The provisions of the current law provide for the transfer of municipal and district tasks in the field of public collective transport to a metropolitan union. In the area of public collective transport, the main statutory task of the association will be: planning, coordination, integration and development of public collective transport, as well as metropolitan passenger transport. The union will not deprive municipalities of transport tasks under the law - it will not be necessary to liquidate or transform the Communication Union of the Upper Silesian Industrial District or other carriers/organizers. The metropolitan union will be able to act as transport organiser for municipal tasks only on the basis of agreements. The association will act as a transport organizer in the field of metropolitan transport, which are a new category of transport (other than municipal, district and voivodship). In this respect, it will be able to develop new rail and road connections with a range that is important from the point of view of the entire metropolis. In terms of planning, the union will adopt a transport plan in force in the area of the union, i.e. municipalities and inter-communal relationships. It has been clarified that the task of the relationship is social and economic development of the area of the union. It is proposed to reduce bureaucratic procedures related to the decommissioning of one unit and the creation of another. It is proposed that all rights and obligations of the inter-city may be transferred to the metropolitan union by mutual agreement. During the indicated organisational period, the union will gain 20% revenue, which is linked to the need to guarantee resources for organisational, legal and material preparation. The Voivodeship will be able to grant a targeted grant for activities related to its creation. It is proposed that a representative of the implementing body be represented by the local authority concerned. It is proposed to remove their representatives from the assembly (no contributions). In accordance with the provisions in force, the resolutions of the assembly shall in principle be adopted by a qualified majority understood as a majority of the local government units represented in the assembly. It is proposed that this majority be defined by a double majority and that a double majority be applied to all assembly decisions. It is proposed that the management board of the metropolitan union should count five members instead of three to avoid a stalemate. The possibility of assistance to trade unions and the possibility of assistance to the metropolitan union by local government units and their associations is envisaged. The size of the audit committee has been clarified, comprising at least three delegates. In view of the potential difficulties of interpretation with regard to the proper application of the provisions, the draft provision expressly requires members of the board of directors of the union, the treasurer of the union and the secretary to make a declaration of property and has been authorised by the President of the Council of Ministers to define its model by means of a regulation. The possibility for the metropolitan association to acquire movable and immovable property by means of agreements with municipalities associated with, but also with, non-members of, local government units and their associations or bodies representing the State Treasury shall be introduced. Where a transport plan is drawn up by a metropolitan union of the municipality, inter-communal relationships, powiats or associations of powiats whose area is covered by a metropolitan union and which are part of a metropolitan union, no transport plans shall be drawn up. On the date of entry into force of that plan, the transport plans developed by the municipalities, inter-communal unions, powiats or associations of powiats whose area is covered by the metropolitan union and which form part of the metropolitan union shall cease to apply.
